Wait for all 4 agents to complete before proceeding to collect_confirmations. This prevents duplicate work and wasted context.\n277\t\n278\tContinue to collect_confirmations.\n279\t</step>\n280\t\n281\t<step name=\"collect_confirmations\">\n282\tWait for all 4 background agents to finish, then read each agent's output file to collect confirmations.\n283\t\n284\tEach `Agent(...)` call above with `run_in_background=true` returns an `async_launched` result that carries an `outputFile` path (and `canReadOutputFile: true`). The 4 agents run concurrently and each one's completion arrives as a message in this conversation when it finishes — do NOT issue a separate blocking call to wait for them.\n285\t\n286\t**Once all 4 agents have reported completion, read each agent's output file (single message with 4 Read calls):**\n287\t```\n288\tRead tool:\n289\t  file_path: \"{outputFile from that agent's async_launched result}\"\n290\t```\n291\t\n292\t> Allow up to `workflow.subagent_timeout` for the slowest agent to finish before treating it as failed. The timeout is configurable via `workflow.subagent_timeout` in `.planning/config.json` (milliseconds). Default: 300000 (5 minutes). Increase for large codebases or slower models.\n293\t\n294\tEach output file contains that agent's completion confirmation. Parse the confirmation marker (see below) from the file contents.\n295\t\n296\t**Expected confirmation format from each agent:**\n297\t```\n298\t## Mapping Complete\n299\t\n300\t**Focus:** {focus}\n301\t**Documents written:**\n302\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC1}.md` ({N} lines)\n303\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC2}.md` ({N} lines)\n304\t\n305\tReady for orchestrator summary.\n306\t```\n307\t\n308\t**What you receive:** Just file paths and line counts. This replaces `spawn_agents` and `collect_confirmations`.\n317\t\n318\t**IMPORTANT:** Do NOT use `browser_subagent`, `Explore`, or any browser-based tool. Use only file system tools (Read, Bash, Write, Grep, Glob, list_dir, view_file, grep_search, or equivalent tools available in your runtime).\n319\t\n320\t**IMPORTANT:** Set all date stamps (`**Analysis Date:**`, footer, `<!-- refreshed -->`) to `{date}` from init context, overwriting any existing date — Update runs seed from files with concrete prior dates, so merely replacing `[YYYY-MM-DD]` placeholders is not sufficient. NEVER guess the date.\n321\t\n322\t**SCOPE:** When `${PATH_SCOPE_HINT}` is non-empty (i.e. `--paths` was supplied), restrict every pass below to the validated path prefixes in `${SCOPED_PATHS}`. When `${PATH_SCOPE_HINT}` is empty, perform a full-repo scan.\n323\t\n324\tPerform all 4 mapping passes sequentially:\n325\t\n326\t**Pass 1: Tech Focus**\n327\t- Explore package.json/Cargo.toml/go.mod/requirements.txt, config files, dependency trees\n328\t- Write `.planning/codebase/STACK.md` — Languages, runtime, frameworks, dependencies, configuration\n329\t- Write `.planning/codebase/INTEGRATIONS.md` — External APIs, databases, auth providers, webhooks\n330\t\n331\t**Pass 2: Architecture Focus**\n332\t- Explore directory structure, entry points, module boundaries, data flow\n333\t- Write `.planning/codebase/ARCHITECTURE.md` — Pattern, layers, data flow, abstractions, entry points\n334\t- Write `.planning/codebase/STRUCTURE.md` — Directory layout, key locations, naming conventions\n335\t\n336\t**Pass 3: Quality Focus**\n337\t- Explore code style, error handling patterns, test files, CI config\n338\t- Write `.planning/codebase/CONVENTIONS.md` — Code style, naming, patterns, error handling\n339\t- Write `.planning/codebase/TESTING.md` — Framework, structure, mocking, coverage\n340\t\n341\t**Pass 4: Concerns Focus**\n342\t- Explore TODOs, known issues, fragile areas, security patterns\n343\t- Write `.planning/codebase/CONCERNS.md` — Tech debt, bugs, security, performance, fragile areas\n344\t\n345\tUse the same document templates as the `gsd-codebase-mapper` agent. It runs on\n376\tevery mapping run, incremental (`--paths`) and full alike. `--files` narrows it\n377\tto the documents an Update run actually refreshed; `--paths` needs no narrowing\n378\tbecause all seven are regenerated, just scoped in content.\n379\t\n380\t**Why this is a shell step and not an instruction to the mapper.** The stamp is\n381\tthe only machine-readable freshness marker: the `verify codebase-drift` gate\n382\treads it to decide what to diff HEAD against. The human-readable markers the\n383\tmapper writes (`**Analysis Date:**`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) are restamped\n384\tunconditionally on an Update run, so a mapper that decides its work is already\n385\tdone and rewrites only the dates still looks fresh to a human. Leaving the\n386\tmachine-readable stamp to the same agent reproduces exactly the failure the\n387\tstamp exists to detect. A shell step cannot be skipped by a confident agent.\n388\t\n389\tThe command is non-blocking: it emits `skipped` with a `reason` outside a git\n390\trepo or when no documents exist. Report `stamped` and `commit` in the summary\n391\tif any entry in `failed` is non-empty; otherwise continue silently.\n392\t\n393\tRun in this position, before `commit_codebase_map`, the stamp lands on\n394\tdocuments the mapper just wrote, so its markdown whitespace normalization is\n395\tfolded into the same commit. Running `stamp-codebase-map` by hand against an\n396\talready-committed map reflows that map's whitespace as a side effect.\n397\t\n398\tContinue to scan_for_secrets.\n399\t</step>\n400\t\n401\t<step name=\"scan_for_secrets\">\n402\t**CRITICAL SECURITY CHECK:** Scan output files for accidentally leaked secrets before committing.\n403\t\n404\tRun secret pattern detection:\n405\t\n406\t```bash\n407\t# Check for common API key patterns in generated docs\n408\tgrep -E '(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|sk_live_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|sk_test_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|gho_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|glpat-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|AKIA[A-Z0-9]{16}|xox[baprs]-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|[REDACTED:gsd-scan]|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.)' .planning/codebase/*.md 2>/dev/null && SECRETS_FOUND=true || SECRETS_FOUND=false\n```\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=true:**\n\n```\n⚠️  SECURITY ALERT: Potential secrets detected in codebase documents!\n\nFound patterns that look like API keys or tokens in:\n[show grep output]\n\nThis would expose credentials if committed.\n\n**Action required:**\n1. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017CymcV7ckUySkVCrqxkz64","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Architecture\n2\t\n3\tStatus: Accepted\n4\t\n5\tThis document describes how the portfolio site is structured: layers, import rules, folder layout, data flow, the content model, the design-token pipeline and the animation layer. It is written so the scaffold can be built directly from it. Decisions with a longer rationale live in the [ADR log](adr/README.md).\n6\t\n7\t## Overview\n8\t\n9\tThe site is a static, zero-backend, single-page React app served from GitHub Pages. There is no router, no global store and no data fetching. The architecture comes down to four seams:\n10\t\n11\t1. Typed content: all copy lives in TypeScript modules checked against one schema.\n12\t2. CSS-variable design tokens: five role colors per theme drive every surface.\n13\t3. An internal animation layer: the only place the two animation libraries are imported.\n14\t4. A build and deploy pipeline: Vite build, GitHub Actions, GitHub Pages.\n15\t\n16\tEverything else is conventional React composition with props flowing down.\n17\t\n18\t## Layers\n19\t\n20\tDependencies point down only. A layer may import from layers below it, never above.\n21\t\n22\t```text\n23\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n24\t| app/        main.tsx, App.tsx, Providers, Page.tsx                    |\n25\t|             Page.tsx is the ONLY file that imports content data       |\n26\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n27\t| sections/   Hero, About, Experience, Projects, Skills, Education,     |\n28\t|             Contact                      (props in, JSX out)          |\n29\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n30\t| layout/     Container, Section, SiteHeader, PillNav, Footer,          |\n31\t|             SkipLink, Wordmark                                        |\n32\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n33\t| components/ ui/ (CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle, icons)                  |\n34\t|             motion-primitives/ (vendored source, treated as owned)    |\n35\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n36\t| anim/       the internal animation layer: the only place that         |\n37\t|             imports motion/react and @react-spring/web               |\n38\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n39\t| hooks/  lib/                  | styles/ (tokens.css, base.css,       |\n40\t| (useActiveSection, useTheme,  |  index.css = tailwind + theme map)   |\n41\t|  cn, a11y)                    |                                      |\n42\t+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------+\n43\t| content/ (typed TS data)      assets/ (images, fonts via Fontsource) |\n44\t+----------------------------------------------------------------------+\n45\tBuild and deploy: vite.config.ts, lint boundary rules, GitHub Actions\n46\t```\n47\t\n48\t## Import rules\n49\t\n50\tOnly src/anim/ and src/components/motion-primitives/ may import motion/react or @react-spring/web.\n51\t\n52\tOnly src/app/Page.tsx imports content data.\n53\t\n54\t| Importer | May import | May not import |\n55\t|----------|-----------|----------------|\n56\t| sections/* | layout, components/ui, the anim barrel (src/anim/index.ts), content types (not data) | motion/react, @react-spring/*, components/motion-primitives/* directly, content data |\n57\t| layout/*, components/ui/* | the anim barrel, lib, hooks | sections, content data |\n58\t| components/motion-primitives/* | motion/react, lib/cn | everything else (vendor code, kept out of lint autofix churn) |\n59\t| anim/* | motion/react, @react-spring/web, components/motion-primitives/*, the token mirror in styles | sections, layout |\n60\t| app/Page.tsx | content data, sections | any animation library or anim internals |\n61\t\n62\tThese rules are enforced by oxlint's `no-restricted-imports` rule with per-folder overrides in `.oxlintrc.json`, covering dynamic `import()` and re-exports as well, so a violation fails CI rather than relying on review. Unit tests under `tests/unit` may import the libraries for assertions. A gesture library is not adopted up front. It would be added to the anim row only if a react-spring drag cannot be built well otherwise, after a React 19 compatibility check.\n63\t\n64\t## Folder structure\n65\t\n66\t```text\n67\trepo root\n68\t|-- public/                  URL-stable files only: favicon set, og image,\n69\t|                            resume.pdf (a phone-number-free export), robots.txt\n70\t|-- index.html               meta and social tags, inline theme-init script,\n71\t|                            noscript fallback content\n72\t|-- 404.html                 second HTML entry: the not-found page that\n73\t|                            GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths\n74\t|-- vite.config.ts           base from env, Tailwind plugin, imagetools, \"@\" alias\n75\t|-- .oxlintrc.json           import-boundary rules (see Import rules)\n76\t|-- .github/workflows/       ci.yml, deploy.yml\n77\t`-- src/\n78\t    |-- main.tsx\n79\t    |-- app/                 App.tsx, Page.tsx, Providers.tsx\n80\t    |-- sections/            one folder per section (Hero, About, Experience,\n81\t    |                        Projects, Skills, Education, Contact)\n82\t    |-- layout/              Container, Section, SiteHeader, PillNav,\n83\t    |                        Footer, SkipLink, Wordmark\n84\t    |-- components/\n85\t    |   |-- ui/              CtaLink, Toast, ThemeToggle, icons/ (inline SVG\n86\t    |   |                    brand marks and UI glyphs)\n87\t    |   `-- motion-primitives/   vendored, patched locally, logged in an ADR\n88\t    |-- anim/\n89\t    |   |-- index.ts         public barrel: the only animation import path\n90\t    |   |-- MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx\n91\t    |   |-- useMotionPreference.ts\n92\t    |   |-- tokens.ts        springs, durations, eases in both libraries' formats\n93\t    |   |-- MotionFeatures.tsx   LazyMotion provider with the async feature chunk\n94\t    |   |-- declarative/     motion-owned wrappers (ToastPresence,\n95\t    |   |                    NavIndicator, PillMorph, TextReveal)\n96\t    |   `-- physics/         react-spring-owned wrappers (ThemeThumb and other\n97\t    |                        pointer-driven, velocity-carrying helpers)\n98\t    |-- content/\n99\t    |   |-- schema.ts        every type\n100\t    |   |-- profile.ts, experience.ts, projects.ts, skills.ts,\n101\t    |   |   education.ts, contacts.ts     one module per section\n102\t    |   |-- images.ts        image registry\n103\t    |   `-- index.ts         assembles siteContent with satisfies\n104\t    |-- hooks/               useActiveSection, useTheme\n105\t    |-- lib/                 cn.ts, date formatting, a11y helpers\n106\t    |-- styles/\n107\t    |   |-- index.css        tailwind import, dark variant, theme mapping\n108\t    |   |-- tokens.css       role tokens, derived tokens, light and dark blocks\n109\t    |   `-- base.css         resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard, fonts\n110\t    `-- assets/images/       hashed by Vite\n111\t```\n112\t\n113\tThe animation folder is named anim/ rather than motion/ so it is never confused with the motion package or its motion/react import path.\n114\t\n115\t## Data flow\n116\t\n117\tBuild time:\n118\t\n119\t```text\n120\tcontent/*.ts --(typed, satisfies)--> app/Page --props--> sections --props--> layout, ui\n121\tassets/*     --(imagetools, hashed imports)--> image fields in content\n122\t```\n123\t\n124\tRuntime, all client-side with no network use beyond same-origin assets:\n125\t\n126\t- Theme: an inline script in the document head reads localStorage, falls back to prefers-color-scheme, and sets the data-theme attribute on the html element before first paint. The theme provider in React only changes the attribute and storage on a user toggle.\n127\t- Motion preference: the OS reduced-motion setting (plus an optional site override) feeds one hook, useMotionPreference, which configures both animation libraries and the wrapper components. See Reduced motion.\n128\t- Scroll-spy: an IntersectionObserver over the section ids drives useActiveSection, which highlights the current section in the navigation.\n129\t- Pointer and touch input drive react-spring values written straight to the DOM, with no React re-render per frame.\n130\t- Project detail: a click or tap sets local state in the Projects section, which opens an overlay with focus handling.\n131\t\n132\tThere is no global state library. Three small contexts exist: theme, motion preference and, optionally, the active section. Everything else is props.\n133\t\n134\t## Content model\n135\t\n136\tAll types live in src/content/schema.ts:\n137\t\n138\t- Profile: name, tagline, bio paragraphs, optional quote, photo (ImageAsset), contacts.\n139\t- ContactLink: kind (email, github, linkedin or instagram), label, href, whether it is external.\n140\t- ExperienceEntry: id, role, org, optional org URL, optional logo, dates (DateRange), location, bullets, optional highlights and stack.\n141\t- Project: id, title, context kind (personal, employer, course or hackathon) with a label, dates, stack, bullets, relatedExperienceId, featured, optional links and image.\n142\t- EducationEntry: id, degree, school, location, dates, gpa, optional track.\n143\t- SkillGroup: id, label, items as a string array.\n144\t- DateRange: ISO start and end (or present) plus a display label in one normalized format.\n145\t- ImageAsset: src, alt, width, height, optional srcset.\n146\t\n147\tRules:\n148\t\n149\t- Content modules use satisfies against the schema so a missing field fails typecheck.\n150\t- Ordering lives in the data (array order or a priority field), never in components.\n151\t- Copy is taken verbatim from the reviewed content inventory; any edit is recorded there with before and after, not made silently in code.\n152\t- The featured flag decides what appears first on mobile, so \"key info in seconds\" is a data decision.\n153\t- Content tests check that ids are unique, related ids resolve, every image has alt text, every external link is https, and dates parse in descending order.\n154\t- A phone number is not a field anywhere in the model, and must never appear in site copy, metadata or the served resume.\n155\t\n156\t## Design tokens\n157\t\n158\t- Exactly five role tokens per theme: text, background, primary, secondary, accent. Light and dark are authored separately, not inverted.\n159\t- Derived tokens (surface, border, muted text, link, focus ring and so on) are computed from the five roles with color-mix(in oklch, ...). A palette swap therefore edits one block and every derived value follows.\n160\t- The theme is selected by the data-theme attribute on the html element.\n161\t- Tailwind v4 is configured CSS-first: a custom dark variant is bound to [data-theme=dark] with @custom-variant, and an @theme inline block maps utilities to the CSS variables. There is no tailwind.config.js.\n162\t- No raw hex values in components. Components use semantic utilities only; a CI check greps for stray hex.\n163\t- A contrast test recomputes every text, UI and focus pair in both themes against WCAG 2.2 AA (4.5:1 for body text, 3:1 for large text and UI) and fails the build on any miss.\n164\t- Spring and duration values that JavaScript needs are mirrored in CSS variables, with src/anim/tokens.ts as the canonical source for springs.\n165\t\n166\t## Animation layer\n167\t\n168\tsrc/anim/index.ts is the only animation import path for the rest of the app. It exposes a handful of semantic components and hooks, not library primitives.\n169\t\n170\t| Interaction class | Owner |\n171\t|-------------------|-------|\n172\t| Mount and unmount, presence, exit animations | motion |\n173\t| Layout and shared-element transitions | motion |\n174\t| Scroll-linked values and in-view reveals | motion |\n175\t| Text and number effects, stagger, ready-made motion-primitives prefabs | motion |\n176\t| Velocity-carrying physics: press-squish, rail hover lift with neighbor shift, theme toggle thumb | react-spring |\n177\t| Color and hover transitions, native scroll-snap rail swipe | CSS |\n178\t| Desktop wheel smoothing only | Lenis |\n179\t\n180\tThe rule: one library animates any given element. Never both libraries on one element or one property, and no Tailwind transition utilities on an element a library animates. If a vendored primitive duplicates a physics wrapper, one implementation is chosen per interaction and the other stays unused.\n181\t\n182\tSpring and duration tokens are defined once in src/anim/tokens.ts, exported in both libraries' formats (motion's stiffness and damping, react-spring's tension and friction), so the two feel like one system. Fine-pointer effects such as tilt, magnetic pull and spotlight are gated by (hover: hover) and (pointer: fine); touch gets a tap or press equivalent, or nothing. Every wrapper reads the motion preference, renders an instant equivalent when reduced, forwards className, and never hides content in its initial CSS state.\n183\t\n184\t## Reduced motion\n185\t\n186\tOne preference source, useMotionPreference, combines the OS prefers-reduced-motion setting with an optional site override and feeds three consumers:\n187\t\n188\t- motion: the provider renders MotionConfig with its reducedMotion setting. This disables transform and layout animation but not opacity or color, so wrappers handle parallax, scroll-linked and autoplay effects explicitly.\n189\t- react-spring: the provider calls Globals.assign({ skipAnimation }) so every spring jumps to its target; the effect cleans up on change.\n190\t- CSS: a @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) guard in base.css shortens transitions and animations and keeps scroll behavior native.\n191\t\n192\tContent is visible by default and is never hidden until JavaScript runs. Hidden initial states are applied client-side inside wrappers, and a noscript rule guarantees visibility. Pressed, focused and selected states must read without motion (color, outline, shape). Lenis is off under reduced motion. The provider listens for changes to the media query so a live OS toggle takes effect without a reload.\n193\t\n194\t## Assets\n195\t\n196\t- Images live in src/assets/images/ and are imported as modules (hashed, long-cached). vite-imagetools produces AVIF and WebP variants and srcset with intrinsic width and height to prevent layout shift. Below-the-fold images lazy-load; the hero image is high priority.\n197\t- Fonts are self-hosted through Fontsource (variable, latin subset, font-display swap), at most two families. No third-party font CDN.\n198\t- Brand icons (GitHub, LinkedIn, Instagram, email) and UI glyphs (close, chevrons, download) are inline SVG components under src/components/ui/icons/. No icon package is installed. See ADR-0005 and ADR-0010 in the ADR log.\n199\t- The resume PDF served from public/ is the owner's phone-number-free export of the resume. The phone-number gate reads its text layer, link annotations and metadata, and rejects a stand-in file.\n200\t- Only images that are actually used migrate from the previous site, with EXIF metadata stripped. Template leftovers are left behind.\n201\t\n202\t## Build and deploy\n203\t\n204\tGitHub Actions builds the site and publishes it to GitHub Pages. The Vite base comes from the VITE_BASE environment variable: the project URL path (for example /aa-phan-portfolio-v2/) while staging, and / after the cutover to the user-site URL. Assets are module-imported so no absolute asset paths exist; anything under public/ that JavaScript references goes through import.meta.env.BASE_URL. CI also builds with the project-path base so a broken sub-path is caught before launch. The build has a second HTML entry, 404.html, a static not-found page that GitHub Pages serves for unknown paths. Steps, settings, cutover and rollback are in the deploy runbook (deploy-runbook.md).\n205\t\n206\t## Quality gates\n207\t\n208\t| Gate | Command | Fails the build when |\n209\t|------|---------|----------------------|\n210\t| Typecheck | `tsc -b` | A content field is missing or a reference is wrong |\n211\t| Lint with import boundaries | oxlint, warnings denied | A section imports an animation library or content data |\n212\t| Format check | Prettier with Tailwind class sorting | A file is not formatted |\n213\t| Unit tests | Vitest: shell parity, theme logic, motion preference, gate negatives | Any test fails |\n214\t| Path-case check | `scripts/check-path-case.mjs`, run locally and on Linux CI | An import differs in case from the file on disk |\n215\t| Raw color check | `scripts/check-colors.mjs` | Source outside the token file and vendored primitives holds a raw hex, rgb, hsl, oklch or oklab value, or a Tailwind default palette utility |\n216\t| Token contrast test | `scripts/check-contrast.mjs` | Any text, UI or focus pair is below AA in any theme |\n217\t| Build | `vite build` | The Vite build errors |\n218\t| Initial JS budget | `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs` | The module script and modulepreloads exceed 150 kB gzipped |\n219\t| Phone-number check | `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` over the built site | A served file holds a phone-shaped string or a tel: link, or a PDF's text layer, link annotations or metadata does (read with pdfjs-dist), or the PDF cannot be read, or resume.pdf is not a real resume (the owner's name and at least 1500 non-whitespace characters of text) |\n220\t| Image check | `scripts/check-images.mjs` over dist, public and src | A shipped raster is not AVIF or WebP, carries Exif or XMP, or is unreferenced, or a source image is not imported |\n221\t| Smoke tests | Playwright in CI | The hero is missing with JavaScript off, the theme flashes, or content is hidden or a transform animation runs with reduced motion emulated |\n222\t| axe | Later gate | Serious or critical violations in light or dark |\n223\t| Mobile Lighthouse | Later gate | Targets set in the scaffold milestone are missed |\n224\t\n225\t`npm run verify` chains the first eleven rows in that order, and both CI workflows call it.\n226\t\n227\t## Stack\n228\t\n229\tVerified 2026-10-08; re-verify at Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Bootstrap | Mounts React root in StrictMode, imports fonts and global CSS | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App | Composes providers around the page | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Motion preference, lazy motion features, toast context | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | Reads `siteContent`, derives email and other links, owns scroll-spy and section order | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Scroll-spy hook | IntersectionObserver over section ids; exposes `activeId` and `lockTo` | `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` |\n| Theme hook | Reads and writes the theme attribute and `localStorage` | `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src/lib/theme.ts` |\n| Site header and nav | Desktop header nav and mobile phone pill nav | `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `src/layout/PillNav.tsx`, `src/layout/nav-items.ts` |\n| Sections | One component per portfolio section, props in, JSX out | `src/sections/*/` |\n| Projects overlay | Project detail dialog with focus handling | `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`, `src/sections/Projects/Sleeve.tsx` |\n| Toasts | Toast context and rendering | `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts` |\n| Copy email | Clipboard write with mailto fallback | `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`, `src/lib/copy.ts` |\n| Animation layer | Single import surface for both animation libraries | `src/anim/index.ts` |\n| Content | Typed copy and data for every section | `src/content/index.ts`, `src/content/schema.ts` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Static single-page React app, layered, with typed build-time content and no runtime data layer.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- No router, no global store, no data fetching. The page is one `Page` component with anchor-based sections.\n- Dependencies point downward only. Enforced with oxlint `no-restricted-imports` overrides in `.oxlintrc.json`, so violations fail `npm run lint`.\n- Three small React contexts: theme, motion preference, toast. Other state is local or passed as props. Pointer-driven values are written straight to the DOM through react-spring, without per-frame React renders.\n- Design tokens are CSS variables keyed by `data-theme` on `<html>`; components use semantic Tailwind utilities, not raw colors.\n- Content is data, not code: ordering and featured flags live in `src/content/*.ts`.\n\n## Layers\n\n**Bootstrap / App shell:**\n- Purpose: mount the app and wire providers.\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`, `src/app/`\n- Contains: entry, `App`, `Providers`, `Page`.\n- Depends on: `@/anim`, `@/components/ui/Toast`, `@/content`, `@/hooks`, `@/layout`, `@/sections`.\n- Used by: `index.html` (script tag) and `404.html`.\n\n**Sections:**\n- Purpose: render each portfolio section from props.\n- Location: `src/sections/` (folders `Hero`, `About`, `Experience`, `Projects`, `Skills`, `Education`, `Contact`).\n- Contains: presentational components and small local helpers (`src/sections/Projects/project-types.ts`).\n- Depends on: `@/layout`, `@/components/ui`, `@/anim` barrel, content types from `@/content`.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` only.\n\n**Layout:**\n- Purpose: page chrome, navigation, containers.\n- Location: `src/layout/`.\n- Contains: `Container.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `SiteHeader.tsx`, `PillNav.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS` and nav labels).\n- Depends on: `@/anim`, `@/components/ui`, `@/hooks`, `@/lib`.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`, `src/sections/*`.\n\n**Components (ui and vendored):**\n- Purpose: reusable UI primitives and vendored motion primitives.\n- Location: `src/components/ui/` (`CtaLink.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, `useToast.ts`, `icons/`), `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` (vendored, treated as owned code).\n- Depends on: `@/anim` barrel, `@/lib`.\n- Used by: sections and layout.\n\n**Animation layer:**\n- Purpose: one import path for motion and react-spring, with semantic wrappers and reduced-motion handling.\n- Location: `src/anim/`.\n- Contains:\n  - `index.ts` - public barrel. The only animation import path for the rest of the app.\n  - `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `featuresStatus.ts` - reduced-motion and feature status.\n  - `MotionFeatures.tsx`, `features.ts` - LazyMotion provider with async feature chunk.\n  - `tokens.ts` - spring and duration tokens in both libraries' formats.\n  - `declarative/` - motion-owned wrappers: `ToastPresence.tsx`, `NavIndicator.tsx`, `PillMorph.tsx`, `TextReveal.tsx`.\n  - `physics/ThemeThumb.tsx` - react-spring-owned wrapper.\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`, `components/motion-primitives`, the token mirror in `src/styles`.\n- Used by: sections, layout, components, app providers.\n\n**Hooks and lib:**\n- Purpose: shared logic with no JSX.\n- Location: `src/hooks/` (`useActiveSection.ts`, `useTheme.ts`), `src/lib/` (`theme.ts`, `copy.ts`, `utils.ts`).\n- Depends on: browser APIs (`localStorage`, `matchMedia`, IntersectionObserver, `navigator.clipboard`).\n\n**Content:**\n- Purpose: all site copy and structured data.\n- Location: `src/content/`.\n- Contains: `schema.ts` (types), one module per section (`profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`), `images.ts` (image registry), `index.ts` (assembles `siteContent`).\n- Depends on: `src/assets/images/` through imagetools imports.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` only (plus type-only imports elsewhere).\n\n**Styles:**\n- Purpose: design tokens and global CSS.\n- Location: `src/styles/`.\n- Contains: `tokens.css` (five role colors per theme, derived tokens via `color-mix(in oklch, ...)`), `base.css` (resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard, fonts), `index.css` (Tailwind import, `@custom-variant` for `[data-theme=dark]`, `@theme inline`), `interactions.css`, `not-found.css`.\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (page render)\n\n1. Browser loads `index.html`. An inline script in the head reads `localStorage` key `theme`, falls back to `prefers-color-scheme`, and sets `data-theme` on `<html>` before first paint (`index.html`).\n2. `src/main.tsx` imports fonts and `src/styles/index.css`, then calls `createRoot(...).render(<StrictMode><App/></StrictMode>)`.\n3. `src/app/App.tsx` renders `Providers` around `Page`.\n4. `src/app/Providers.tsx` wraps children in `MotionPreferenceProvider` (`src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`), `MotionFeatures` (`src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`), and `ToastProvider` (`src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`).\n5. `src/app/Page.tsx` reads `siteContent` from `src/content/index.ts`, derives `email` from the `mailto:` entry, and calls `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)`.\n6. `Page` renders `SiteHeader`, `<main id=\"main\">` with `Hero`, `Experience`, `Projects`, `About`, `Skills`, `Education`, `Contact`, then `Footer` and `PillNav`, passing props only.\n\n### Scroll-spy path\n\n1. `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` observes section elements by id using IntersectionObserver.\n2. It returns `activeId` to `Page`, which passes it to `SiteHeader` and `PillNav` so the current section is highlighted.\n3. Navigation calls `lockTo(id)` from the same hook, which suppresses scroll-spy updates during programmatic scrolls.\n\n### Theme toggle path\n\n1. `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx` calls `useTheme` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`).\n2. `src/lib/theme.ts` writes the new value to `localStorage` and updates `data-theme` on `<html>`.\n3. `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx` animates the toggle thumb with react-spring.\n\n### Project detail path\n\n1. A click or tap in `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx` or `Sleeve.tsx` sets local state.\n2. `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` renders the overlay with focus handling. Per `docs/adr/0009-native-dialog-for-project-details.md`, the dialog uses the native `<dialog>` element.\n\n### Reduced-motion path\n\n1. `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` combines `prefers-reduced-motion` with an optional site override.\n2. `MotionPreferenceProvider` sets motion's `MotionConfig` reducedMotion and calls react-spring `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation })`.\n3. `src/styles/base.css` holds a `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` guard for CSS transitions.\n\n**State Management:**\n- Theme: React context in `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` plus `data-theme` attribute and `localStorage`.\n- Motion preference: React context in `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`.\n- Toasts: React context in `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`.\n- Active section: local hook state in `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`.\n- Everything else: props.\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**Content schema:**\n- Purpose: single typed model for all copy and data.\n- Examples: `src/content/schema.ts` (`Profile`, `ContactLink`, `ExperienceEntry`, `Project`, `EducationEntry`, `SkillGroup`, `DateRange`, `ImageAsset`, `Writeup`).\n- Pattern: content modules use `satisfies` against the schema, so a missing field fails `tsc -b`.\n\n**Animation facade:**\n- Purpose: keep library choice out of feature code.\n- Examples: `src/anim/index.ts` (barrel), `src/anim/declarative/PillMorph.tsx`, `src/anim/declarative/ToastPresence.tsx`, `src/anim/physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`.\n- Pattern: each wrapper reads the motion preference, renders an instant equivalent when reduced, forwards `className`, and never hides content in its initial CSS state.\n\n**Design token pipeline:**\n- Purpose: palette swap in one place.\n- Examples: `src/styles/tokens.css`, `src/anim/tokens.ts` (spring values, canonical source).\n- Pattern: five role tokens per theme (text, background, primary, secondary, accent). Derived tokens use `color-mix(in oklch, ...)`.\n\n**Section ids and nav:**\n- Purpose: one list drives scroll-spy and both nav bars.\n- Examples: `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`).\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Application entry:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx` (bundled from `index.html`).\n- Triggers: browser page load.\n- Responsibilities: mount React root, import global styles and font.\n\n**Not-found entry:**\n- Location: `404.html` (second Vite input in `vite.config.ts`, `build.rolldownOptions.input.notFound`).\n- Triggers: GitHub Pages serving unknown paths.\n- Responsibilities: static not-found page styled by `src/styles/not-found.css`.\n\n**Build and dev:**\n- `vite.config.ts` - `npm run dev`, `npm run build`, `npm run preview`.\n- `vitest.config.ts` - `npm test`.\n- `playwright.config.ts` - `npm run test:e2e`.\n\n**Gate scripts (CI entry points, not shipped):**\n- `scripts/check-*.mjs` - invoked by `npm run verify` and `.github/workflows/*.yml`.\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers, no service worker.\n- **Global state:** Module-level values only: `src/anim/tokens.ts` (constants), `src/content/index.ts` (`siteContent` const object). Document-level state lives on `<html data-theme>` and `localStorage`.\n- **Circular imports:** None documented. Import direction is enforced by oxlint rules in `.oxlintrc.json`.\n- **Import boundaries (enforced):**\n  - Only `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/` may import `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web`.\n  - Only `src/app/Page.tsx` may import content data.\n  - Sections may not import content data or animation libraries directly.\n- **Build base path:** Every runtime URL to `public/` must go through `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` (`vite.config.ts` sets `base` from `VITE_BASE`).\n- **Content visibility:** Content must be visible without JavaScript. Hidden initial states are applied client-side inside wrappers only, with a noscript rule in `index.html`.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Importing animation libraries from a section or layout file\n\n**What happens:** A component imports `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` directly, or imports from `src/components/motion-primitives/` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** Breaks the single animation facade and the reduced-motion handling. `npm run lint` fails through the `no-restricted-imports` rules in `.oxlintrc.json`.\n**Do this instead:** Add or use a wrapper exported from `src/anim/index.ts` (see `src/anim/declarative/` and `src/anim/physics/`).\n\n### Reading content data outside `Page.tsx`\n\n**What happens:** A section or layout component imports from `src/content/index.ts` or a module such as `src/content/projects.ts`.\n**Why it's wrong:** Violates the documented rule that only `src/app/Page.tsx` reads data. It couples presentation to the data source and breaks the props-only data flow.\n**Do this instead:** Receive data as props from `src/app/Page.tsx`. Type-only imports from `src/content/schema.ts` or `src/content/index.ts` are allowed.\n\n### Hard-coded asset paths\n\n**What happens:** A URL such as `/resume.pdf` is written into JSX.\n**Why it's wrong:** Breaks under the project-path base `/aa-phan-portfolio-v2/` used by CI.\n**Do this instead:** Use `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}resume.pdf` as in `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`.\n\n### Raw colors in components\n\n**What happens:** Hex, rgb, hsl, oklch values or Tailwind default palette utilities appear in component files.\n**Why it's wrong:** `scripts/check-colors.mjs` fails `npm run verify`, and theme swaps stop working.\n**Do this instead:** Use semantic utilities mapped in `src/styles/index.css` to variables from `src/styles/tokens.css`.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Defensive UI with no error boundary or global error reporter in the repository. Browser-API calls are guarded for availability.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- Clipboard: `src/lib/copy.ts` checks `navigator.clipboard?.writeText` before use, then falls back to a `mailto:` open path in `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`.\n- Storage: `src/lib/theme.ts` reads and writes `localStorage` for the theme key; the inline script in `index.html` guards the read so a blocked storage does not break first paint.\n- Build gates: failures are reported by non-zero exit from `scripts/check-*.mjs`, which fail CI.\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None in the runtime bundle. Only gate scripts print output.\n**Validation:** Compile-time via `satisfies` against `src/content/schema.ts`; content tests in `tests/unit/content.test.ts` check unique ids, resolvable related ids, alt text, https external links, and descending dates.\n**Authentication:** Not applicable.\n**Accessibility:** Skip link (`src/layout/SkipLink.tsx`), focus management in `src/main` landmark (`<main id=\"main\" tabIndex={-1}>` in `src/app/Page.tsx`), reduced-motion handling in `src/anim/`, contrast gate `scripts/check-contrast.mjs`.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2454,"cache_read_input_tokens":52061,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2454,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01CtGZbUQLhn7gZ1Zm93KiTr","agent_id":"a4e3504e09eb260f0","session_id":"ab4e13ac-f50a-486e-ab6b-ec61fffb8076","uuid":"d00a2cba-2a87-4992-a6c9-837ece011792","timestamp":"2026-11-20T04:21:13.692Z","request_id":"req_011ChDYDCAGJrZi6Nx8wjbE2","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Refresh tech and arch codebase map"}

# ci.yml (verify + e2e), deploy.yml (Pages deploy)\n├── .avarice/              # local tooling config (config.json)\n├── .claude/CLAUDE.md      # project instructions for the coding agent\n├── ignore list\n├── prettier.config.mjs    # formatter config\n└── .prettierignore        # formatter ignore list\n```\n\n## Directory Purposes\n\n**`src/app/`:**\n- Purpose: application shell and composition.\n- Contains: `App.tsx`, `Providers.tsx`, `Page.tsx`.\n- Key files: `src/app/Page.tsx` (the only file that reads content data), `src/app/Providers.tsx` (provider order).\n\n**`src/sections/`:**\n- Purpose: one folder per portfolio section.\n- Contains: `Hero/Hero.tsx`, `About/About.tsx`, `Experience/Experience.tsx`, `Projects/` (`Projects.tsx`, `ProjectDialog.tsx`, `Sleeve.tsx`, `project-types.ts`), `Skills/Skills.tsx`, `Education/Education.tsx`, `Contact/Contact.tsx`.\n- Key files: `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` (detail overlay).\n\n**`src/layout/`:**\n- Purpose: page chrome and navigation.\n- Contains: `SiteHeader.tsx`, `PillNav.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `Container.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `nav-items.ts`.\n- Key files: `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`, nav labels).\n\n**`src/components/`:**\n- Purpose: reusable components.\n- Contains:\n  - `ui/` - `CtaLink.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, `useToast.ts`.\n  - `ui/icons/` - inline SVG components: `ChevronLeftIcon.tsx`, `ChevronRightIcon.tsx`, `CloseIcon.tsx`, `DownloadIcon.tsx`, `GitHubIcon.tsx`, `InstagramIcon.tsx`, `LinkedInIcon.tsx`.\n  - `motion-primitives/` - `text-effect.tsx`, vendored and patched locally.\n\n**`src/anim/`:**\n- Purpose: the only place that imports `motion/react` and `@react-spring/web`.\n- Contains: `index.ts` (barrel), `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `MotionFeatures.tsx`, `features.ts`, `featuresStatus.ts`, `tokens.ts`, `declarative/` (`ToastPresence.tsx`, `NavIndicator.tsx`, `PillMorph.tsx`, `TextReveal.tsx`), `physics/` (`ThemeThumb.tsx`).\n- Key files: `src/anim/index.ts` (import path for everything else), `src/anim/tokens.ts` (spring values).\n\n**`src/content/`:**\n- Purpose: typed site copy and data.\n- Contains: `schema.ts`, `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`, `index.ts`.\n- Key files: `src/content/schema.ts` (all types), `src/content/index.ts` (`siteContent` assembly).\n\n**`src/hooks/`:**\n- Purpose: stateful browser hooks.\n- Contains: `useActiveSection.ts` (scroll-spy with `lockTo`), `useTheme.ts`.\n\n**`src/lib/`:**\n- Purpose: pure helpers.\n- Contains: `theme.ts` (theme storage and attribute), `copy.ts` (clipboard), `utils.ts` (class composition with clsx and tailwind-merge).\n\n**`src/styles/`:**\n- Purpose: tokens and global CSS.\n- Contains: `tokens.css` (role tokens, light and dark blocks), `base.css` (resets, focus ring, reduced-motion guard), `index.css` (Tailwind entry, custom variant, `@theme inline` map), `interactions.css`, `not-found.css`.\n\n**`src/assets/images/`:**\n- Purpose: source images, imported as modules.\n- Contains: `about-dj.jpg`, `capitalone.png`, `spectrum.png`, `symbotic.png`.\n\n**`src/types/`:**\n- Purpose: ambient type declarations. Contains `imagetools.d.ts`.\n\n**`tests/`:**\n- Purpose: all automated tests except the build gates.\n- Contains:\n  - `tests/unit/` - `*.test.ts` and `*.test.tsx` (theme, motion preference, content, copy, tokens, layout, gates, boundaries, active section).\n  - `tests/e2e/` - Playwright specs (`smoke.spec.ts`, `hero.spec.ts`, `nav.spec.ts`, `theme.spec.ts`, `motion.spec.ts`, `resume.spec.ts`, `copy-email.spec.ts`, `not-found.spec.ts`, `wordmark.spec.ts`, `content.spec.ts`), `global-setup.ts`, and `mobile/` specs.\n  - `tests/fixtures/` - fixture components used by boundary tests (`anim/`, `app/`, `motion-primitives/`, `sections/`).\n  - `tests/setup.ts` - Vitest setup.\n\n**`scripts/`:**\n- Purpose: build gates run by `npm run verify`.\n- Contains: `check-js-budget.mjs`, `check-no-phone.mjs`, `check-colors.mjs`, `check-contrast.mjs`, `check-path-case.mjs`, `check-images.mjs`, `check-qa-shots.mjs`, `qa-extra-shots.mjs`, `contrast-pairs.json`, `lib/color.mjs`, `lib/tokens.mjs`.\n\n**`.planning/`:**\n- Purpose: GSD workflow state. Not shipped in the build.\n- Contains: `PROJECT.md`, `REQUIREMENTS.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, `STATE.md`, `config.json`, `state.json`, `WINDOWS.md`, `phases/`, `quick/`, `research/`, `codebase/`.\n\n## Key File Locations\n\n**Entry Points:**\n- `index.html`: main HTML entry; inline theme-init script in `<head>`.\n- `404.html`: not-found HTML entry.\n- `src/main.tsx`: React bootstrap.\n\n**Configuration:**\n- `vite.config.ts`: Vite base (`VITE_BASE`), plugins, alias, inputs.\n- `package.json`: scripts and dependency versions.\n- `.oxlintrc.json`: import-boundary rules.\n- `prettier.config.mjs`: formatting.\n- `tsconfig.json`: project references.\n- `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`: Pages deploy; sets `VITE_BASE`.\n\n**Core Logic:**\n- `src/app/Page.tsx`: page composition and data entry point.\n- `src/anim/index.ts`: animation facade.\n- `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`: scroll-spy.\n- `src/lib/theme.ts`: theme persistence.\n\n**Testing:**\n- `tests/unit/`: Vitest unit and component tests.\n- `tests/e2e/`: Playwright end-to-end tests.\n- `vitest.config.ts`, `playwright.config.ts`: runner config.\n\n**Documentation:**\n- `docs/architecture.md`: architecture spec (layers, import rules, token pipeline).\n- `docs/adr/`: decision log.\n- `docs/deploy-runbook.md`: deploy and cutover steps.\n- `DESIGN.md`: design notes.\n\n## Naming Conventions\n\n**Files:**\n- React components: PascalCase `.tsx` matching the export name, for example `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` exports `Hero`, `src/layout/PillNav.tsx` exports `PillNav`.\n- Hooks: `use`-prefixed camelCase `.ts`, for example `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`.\n- Content and utility modules: lowercase or kebab-case `.ts`, for example `src/content/profile.ts`, `src/layout/nav-items.ts`, `src/sections/Projects/project-types.ts`.\n- Icons: `<Name>Icon.tsx` under `src/components/ui/icons/`.\n- Tests: `*.test.ts` / `*.test.tsx` under `tests/unit/`, `*.spec.ts` under `tests/e2e/`.\n- Gate scripts: `check-<thing>.mjs` under `scripts/`.\n- Note: `src/lib/` contains `utils.ts`, not `cn.ts`, although `docs/architecture.md` lists `cn.ts`.\n\n**Directories:**\n- Sections: PascalCase folder per section under `src/sections/` (`Hero`, `About`, `Projects`).\n- Other `src/` folders: lowercase single words (`app`, `layout`, `anim`, `lib`, `hooks`, `styles`, `content`).\n- Exception: `src/components/motion-primitives/` uses kebab-case, as vendored code.\n\n**Imports:**\n- Use the `@/` alias for everything under `src/`, for example `import { Page } from '@/app/Page'`.\n- Import paths must match file-name case exactly. `scripts/check-path-case.mjs` enforces this in CI.\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New portfolio section:**\n- Component: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx`, props in, JSX out.\n- Data: `src/content/<name>.ts`, typed with `satisfies` against types in `src/content/schema.ts`, and added to `siteContent` in `src/content/index.ts`.\n- Wiring: render it from `src/app/Page.tsx` and add its id to `SECTION_IDS` in `src/layout/nav-items.ts`.\n- Tests: `tests/unit/` for logic, `tests/e2e/` for the rendered section.\n\n**New content field:**\n- Type: `src/content/schema.ts`. Data: the relevant `src/content/*.ts` module. Copy must also be recorded in the content inventory under `.planning/phases/01-decisions-and-documentation/` (see that phase's `01-CONTENT-INVENTORY.md`).\n\n**New layout or chrome component:**\n- Implementation: `src/layout/<Name>.tsx`.\n\n**New reusable UI component:**\n- Implementation: `src/components/ui/<Name>.tsx`. Icons go in `src/components/ui/icons/` as inline SVG (see `docs/adr/0005-inline-svg-brand-icons.md`, `docs/adr/0010-inline-svg-ui-glyphs.md`).\n\n**New animation:**\n- Wrapper: `src/anim/declarative/` (motion) or `src/anim/physics/` (react-spring).
